([CyberBoyAyush/zenox-claude](https://github.com/CyberBoyAyush/zenox-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/librarian.md))
- **librarian-agent**: Maintains repository documentation truth, conducts sanitized local-first research, and preserves durable findings and AI contract inventories. ([islamu-ngo/Event](https://github.com/islamu-ngo/Event/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/librarian-agent.md) · ★7)
- **librarian-agents-guide**: AutoBot includes several specialized librarian agents that act as intelligent assistants for information management, research, and knowledge base operations. These agents automatically help users by searching, organizing, and providing relevant information. ([mrveiss/AutoBot-AI](https://github.com/mrveiss/AutoBot-AI/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/librarian-agents-guide.md) · ★11)
- **librarian-analyst**: Self-improving auditor for the librarian. Audits BOTH the library's retrieval health (are descriptions causing the right knowledge to load? dead/overlapping entries? description thrashing via the audit history?) AND the librarian itself (capture quality, taxonomy health, and the curator/description heuristics). Reads the librarian self-memory first, detects oscillation from the audit logs, and proposes gated improvements. Read-only — it proposes, it never writes to the library or to the librarian's own files. ([saralafleur/ember-claude-toolkit](https://github.com/saralafleur/ember-claude-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/librarian/agents/librarian-analyst.md) · ★4 · 4 copies)
- **librarian-archivist**: The SOLE writer for the librarian. After the user approves, it commits knowledge entries under the library, regenerates the Table of Contents from entry frontmatter, applies approved description/taxonomy/self-improvement changes, and records everything in the audit logs (content changes and librarian self-changes). It also performs first-run library scaffolding and repo wire-up. Never decides what to write — it applies only what was approved, flawlessly and in full. ([saralafleur/ember-claude-toolkit](https://github.com/saralafleur/ember-claude-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/librarian/.github/agents/librarian-archivist.agent.md) · ★4 · 4 copies)
- **librarian-critic**: Literature quality critic. Reviews the Librarian's annotated bibliography for coverage gaps, journal quality, scope calibration, recency, and categorization quality. Paired critic for the Librarian. ([brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills/blob/HEAD/skills/16-hsantanna88-clo-author/dot-claude/agents/librarian-critic.md) · ★3.9k · 9 copies)
- **librarian-curator**: Knowledge extractor for the librarian. Reads a conversation/work session, distills the DURABLE knowledge worth keeping (how a system works, how we accomplished something reusable, architectural decisions and direction, how a solution solved a problem, conventions/best practices), de-dupes it against the existing library, classifies each into domain/subdomain/topic, and drafts entries WITH a strong retrieval description. Read-only — it proposes, it never writes to the library. ([saralafleur/ember-claude-toolkit](https://github.com/saralafleur/ember-claude-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/librarian/gemini/agents/librarian-curator.md) · ★4 · 4 copies)

Never fixes what it finds. Use as the verification lane of a herdr orchestration run, and as the profile behind the code gate. ([liemle3893/lirbox](https://github.com/liemle3893/lirbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/lirbox/agents/lirbox-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **lirbox-web-verifier**: Verifies a UI-touching WEB change end-to-end — writes runnable E2E specs (Playwright preferred) for criteria that can be asserted, and captures evidence (screenshots, console/network logs, responsive viewport matrix) for criteria that must be judged. Engine-pluggable with graceful fallback (Playwright → browser-MCP → OS-scripted), honoring a frozen engine chain when the caller supplies one. Use as the web half of a frontend verification gate in a delivery workflow, or standalone to verify a web change. Distinguishes tooling failure from app failure; never silently passes. ([liemle3893/lirbox](https://github.com/liemle3893/lirbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/lirbox/agents/lirbox-web-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **lisa-architecture-specialist**: Architecture specialist agent. Designs implementation approaches, traces data flow, identifies files to modify, maps dependencies, finds reusable code, evaluates design patterns, and flags breaking changes. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-architecture-specialist.md))
- **lisa-bug-fixer**: Bug fix agent. Reproduces bugs as failing tests, implements fixes via TDD, and verifies the fix resolves the issue without introducing regressions. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-bug-fixer.md))
- **lisa-builder**: Feature build agent. Translates acceptance criteria into tests, implements features via TDD, and verifies all criteria are met. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-builder.md))
- **lisa-confluence-prd-intake**: PRD intake agent for Confluence-hosted PRDs. Runs one intake cycle against a Confluence space or parent page — claims PRDs carrying the configured `ready` label (relabels to the configured `in_review` label), validates each through the dry-run pipeline, and routes to the configured `blocked` label (with clarifying comments) or `ticketed` label (with destination tickets created). Confluence counterpart of `notion-prd-intake`. Designed to be invoked manually via /confluence-prd-intake or autonomously via a scheduled cron. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-confluence-prd-intake.md))
- **lisa-debug-specialist**: Debug specialist agent. Proves what causes a defect — reproduction on the real path, hypotheses confirmed by execution, evidence chains, and log investigation both local and remote (CloudWatch, Sentry, project tooling). Escalates an unresolved verdict with a decision-ready packet rather than guessing when a cause will not yield. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-debug-specialist.md))
- **lisa-eval-specialist**: Evaluation specialist agent. Measures the factory itself rather than the software it produces — maintains the entity's own task suite, samples it against a recorded baseline to catch capability decline, and reports delivery effectiveness alongside autonomy. Independent of the agents it judges by construction. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-eval-specialist.md))
- **lisa-git-history-analyzer**: Analyzes git commit history and pull request context to document the decision-making process behind file changes. Use when you need to understand why and how files evolved over time. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-git-history-analyzer.md))
- **lisa-github-agent**: GitHub Issues lifecycle agent. Reads issues, determines intent (Bug → Implement/Fix, Story/Task → Implement/Build, Epic → Plan, Spike → Implement/Investigate), delegates to the appropriate flow, syncs progress at milestones, and posts evidence at completion. The GitHub counterpart of jira-agent. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-github-agent.md))
- **lisa-github-build-intake**: GitHub build-intake agent. Runs one build-intake cycle against a GitHub repository — claims issues carrying the configured `ready` build label, dispatches each to the github-agent build flow, relabels to the configured (env-aware) `done` label on success. Symmetric counterpart to jira-build-intake. Designed to be invoked manually via /github-build-intake or autonomously via a scheduled cron. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-github-build-intake.md))
- **lisa-github-prd-intake**: PRD intake agent for GitHub Issues hosted PRDs. Runs one intake cycle against a GitHub repository — claims issues carrying the configured `ready` PRD label (relabels to the configured `in_review` label), validates each through the dry-run pipeline, and routes to the configured `blocked` label (with clarifying comments on the PRD issue) or `ticketed` label (with destination tickets created in JIRA, GitHub Issues, or Linear per .lisa.config.json). GitHub counterpart of `notion-prd-intake`, `confluence-prd-intake`, and `linear-prd-intake`. Designed to be invoked manually via /github-prd-intake o… ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-github-prd-intake.md))
- **lisa-jira-agent**: JIRA lifecycle agent. Reads tickets, determines intent (Bug → Implement/Fix, Story/Task → Implement/Build, Epic → Plan, Spike → Implement/Investigate), delegates to the appropriate flow, syncs progress at milestones, and posts evidence at completion. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-jira-agent.md))
- **lisa-jira-build-intake**: JIRA build-intake agent. Runs one build-intake cycle against a JIRA project (or JQL filter) — claims tickets in the configured `ready` status, dispatches each to the jira-agent build flow, transitions to the configured (env-aware) `done` status on success. Symmetric counterpart to notion-prd-intake. Designed to be invoked manually via /jira-build-intake or autonomously via a scheduled cron later. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-jira-build-intake.md))
- **lisa-learner**: Post-implementation learning agent. Capture-only — collects task learnings, builds eight-field entries, and persists them to the machine-managed ledger through the executable contract with provenance. Never promotes: it creates no skills, appends no rules, files no upstream issues; promotion is exclusively the gardener's ticket-gated job. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-learner.md))
- **lisa-learning-judge**: Skeptical judgment gate for candidate learnings. Classifies each candidate as durable-learning, one-off, misunderstanding/spec-gap, or lisa-upstream with mandatory evidence citation. Hostile default — most candidates are DROPPED; only durable-learning ever persists. Use whenever a failure signal or debrief produces a claimed learning that something wants to write to the project learnings surface. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-learning-judge.md))
- **lisa-learnings-synthesizer**: Learnings synthesizer for the Debrief flow. Consumes the parallel outputs of tracker-mining-specialist and pr-mining-specialist, deduplicates, categorizes each candidate into one of {edge case, recurring gotcha, process friction, tooling gap, convention drift, decomposition infidelity, prd defect, missing tool access}, and produces the human-triage document. Exhaustive — surfaces every candidate, even low-confidence ones, because the human decides what to keep. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-learnings-synthesizer.md))
- **lisa-linear-agent**: Linear lifecycle agent. Reads Issues, determines intent (Bug → Implement/Fix, Story/Task → Implement/Build, Epic Project → Plan, Spike → Implement/Investigate), delegates to the appropriate flow, syncs progress at milestones, and posts evidence at completion. Linear counterpart of jira-agent and github-agent. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-linear-agent.md))
- **lisa-linear-build-intake**: Linear build-intake agent. Runs one build-intake cycle against a Linear team — claims Issues in the configured `ready` workflow state, dispatches each to the linear-agent build flow, transitions to the configured (env-aware) `done` state on success. Symmetric counterpart of jira-build-intake and github-build-intake. Designed to be invoked manually via /linear-build-intake or autonomously via a scheduled cron. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-linear-build-intake.md))
- **lisa-linear-prd-intake**: PRD intake agent for Linear-hosted PRDs. Runs one intake cycle against a Linear workspace or team — claims projects carrying the configured `ready` PRD label (relabels to the configured `in_review` label), validates each through the dry-run pipeline, and routes to the configured `blocked` label (with clarifying comments on the project) or `ticketed` label (with destination tickets created). Linear counterpart of `notion-prd-intake` and `confluence-prd-intake`. Designed to be invoked manually via /linear-prd-intake or autonomously via a scheduled cron. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-linear-prd-intake.md))
- **lisa-notion-prd-intake**: PRD intake agent. Runs one intake cycle against a Notion PRD database — claims PRDs in the configured `ready` status, validates each through the dry-run pipeline, and routes to the `blocked` status (with clarifying comments) or the `ticketed` status (with destination tickets created). Designed to be invoked manually via /notion-prd-intake or autonomously via a scheduled cron. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-notion-prd-intake.md))
- **lisa-ops-specialist**: Operations specialist agent for Rails applications deployed with Kamal on ECS Fargate. Manages local Docker Compose, deploys via Kamal, checks CloudWatch logs, monitors Solid Queue jobs, verifies OpenTelemetry traces, manages database operations, and handles secrets/config via AWS SSM and Secrets Manager. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-ops-specialist.md))
- **lisa-performance-specialist**: Performance specialist agent. Identifies N+1 queries, inefficient algorithms, memory leaks, missing indexes, unnecessary re-renders, bundle size issues, and other software performance problems. Recommends optimizations with evidence. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-performance-specialist.md))
- **lisa-pr-mining-specialist**: PR mining specialist for the Debrief flow. Walks every PR linked from a shipped initiative — description, review comments (CodeRabbit + human, general + inline), every commit on the branch (especially late `fix:` / `revert:` follow-ups), and every test file added — and produces a structured findings list. Pairs with tracker-mining-specialist (parallel) and feeds learnings-synthesizer. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-pr-mining-specialist.md))
- **lisa-product-specialist**: Product/UX specialist agent. Defines user flows in Gherkin, writes acceptance criteria from user perspective, identifies UX concerns and error states, and empirically verifies behavior matches requirements. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-product-specialist.md))
- **lisa-quality-specialist**: Code quality specialist agent. Reviews correctness, coding philosophy compliance (immutability, function structure), test coverage, and documentation. Explains findings in beginner-friendly plain English, ranked by severity. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-quality-specialist.md))
- **lisa-security-specialist**: Security specialist agent. Performs threat modeling (STRIDE), reviews code for OWASP Top 10 vulnerabilities, checks auth/validation/secrets handling, and recommends mitigations. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-security-specialist.md))
- **lisa-skill-evaluator**: Six-rung ladder router for candidate learnings. Given a candidate (rule, why, provenance, evidence) it recommends a destination rung — EXECUTABLE-CONTROL | EAGER-RULE | SKILL | WIKI | KEEP-IN-LEDGER | RETIRE — and a scope (project | upstream), with a plain-language rationale and a drafted artifact. Advisory-only — it writes nothing and files nothing; the gardener turns its recommendations into human-gated tracker tickets. Use whenever a flow needs routing judgment about where a piece of knowledge should live. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-skill-evaluator.md))
- **lisa-spec-conformance-specialist**: Spec conformance specialist agent. Verifies shipped work matches its spec exactly — acceptance criteria, Out of Scope, Technical Approach, Validation Journey assertions, and deliverables. Produces a coverage matrix, flags scope creep separately from misses, and issues a verdict (CONFORMS / PARTIAL / DIVERGES). Runs alongside verification-specialist and product-specialist during the verification phase; they catch different failure modes. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-spec-conformance-specialist.md))
- **lisa-test-specialist**: Test specialist agent. Designs test strategy (matrix, edge cases, coverage targets, TDD sequence), writes comprehensive unit and integration tests, and reviews test quality. Tests behavior, not implementation details. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-test-specialist.md))
- **lisa-tracker-mining-specialist**: Tracker mining specialist for the Debrief flow. Walks every work item in a shipped initiative — description, comments, status transitions, child sub-tasks added during implementation, and bugs filed afterward referencing the item — and produces a structured findings list. Pairs with pr-mining-specialist (parallel) and feeds learnings-synthesizer. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-tracker-mining-specialist.md))
- **lisa-verification-specialist**: Verification specialist agent. Discovers project tooling and executes verification for all required types. Plans and executes empirical proof that work is done by running the actual system and observing results. ([CodySwannGT/railsstarter](https://github.com/CodySwannGT/railsstarter/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/lisa-verification-specialist.md))

Liskov — Turing Award laureate, originator of the Liskov Substitution Principle, foundational work on abstract data types (CLU), modular programming, distributed-system primitives, and decades of teaching software engineering at MIT. - **lit-reviewer**: 真实文献调研专用。给定一个生态学/行为学/演化生物学/人工生命的问题，负责实际检索（WebSearch/WebFetch）、筛选、精读，产出**可直接并入 docs/&lt;topic&gt;.md 的 [现实] 标签散文段落**，外加每篇的 notes/papers/&lt;citekey&gt;.md 笔记草稿与 lit/literature-log.md 台账行，每条引用附 DOI 或 arXiv ID。以下情况应当派给它：「自然界里 X 是怎样的」「这个设计有没有真实生物学依据」「piosphere / landscape of fear / Weismann barrier 的文献怎么说」「这个想法在 ALife 里有没有人做过」「给 docs/biology.md 补一节」「这条引用靠不靠谱」。它只读不写，返回的是待落盘的草稿，由派活的人写进文件。不要用它分析本地实验结果（result-analyst）或核查已写下的结论（claim-verifier）。 ([Asada-Sinon/Alicization](https://github.com/Asada-Sinon/Alicization/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-reviewer.md))
- **lit-reviewer**: 文献调研专用。给定一个主题、方法名或研究问题，负责实际检索、筛选、精读并产出按主题聚类的结构化综述与研究 gap 列表。以下情况应当派给它：「这个方向已经有哪些工作」「XX 方法最近的进展」「我这个想法有没有人做过」「帮我找几个可比的 baseline」「补一下相关工作」「这篇论文的方法和我们的有什么区别」。不要用它来分析本地实验结果（那是 result-analyst）或核查已写下的结论（那是 claim-verifier）。 ([Asada-Sinon/TEOW](https://github.com/Asada-Sinon/TEOW/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-reviewer.md))
- **lit-reviewer**: Synthesize research notes into themes and argument skeleton (synthesis mode) or research gap map (gap mode). Dispatched by literature-review skill. Not user-invokable directly. ([Avishekdevnath/SRA-Smart-Research-Assistant](https://github.com/Avishekdevnath/SRA-Smart-Research-Assistant/blob/HEAD/agents/lit-reviewer.md))
- **lit-scout**: 文献侦察。多模态并行检索同一问题，返回去重后的证据摘要而非文献列表。当需要了解某个主题的现有工作、查找阴性结果、追溯某个说法的原始出处、或在 KG 覆盖不足时替代图检索时使用。 ([HERRY423/AI-BioResearch-Workflow](https://github.com/HERRY423/AI-BioResearch-Workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-scout.md) · ★2)
- **lite-drafter**: Produces a short, triage-grade paper summary — Key Takeaways, Background, Main Idea & Summary, Critique. Invoked alongside metadata-extractor and finding-extractor during /paperloom:ingest. Returns JSON only; page assembly is handled by scripts/assemble_paper.py. ([trapoom555/claude-paperloom](https://github.com/trapoom555/claude-paperloom/blob/HEAD/agents/lite-drafter.md) · ★95)

Reads an input document, classifies its type, and extracts every load-bearing claim as an atomic proposition tagged problem/solution/assumption/prediction/numeric. Output is JSON conforming to atoms-schema. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-atomizer.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-grounder**: Phase 2 of Litmus, the novel piece. For each atomic claim (especially numeric and prediction atoms), semantically search the web via Claude's inbuilt exa (mcp__claude_ai_Exa_2__*) and produce a citation table classifying the claim as GROUNDED / CONTRADICTED / UNGROUNDED / UNFALSIFIABLE. Uses the bundled Claude exa, not the user's local exa MCP. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-grounder.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-independent-designer**: Phase 1 of Litmus. Receives ONLY problem-statement atoms (NOT the source document) and produces a fresh design for the stated problem. The frame-break: the designer cannot anchor on a document it has never seen. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-independent-designer.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-accountability**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Accountability lens. Identifies who owns this in production, who gets paged, who has authority to roll back, chains of responsibility, oversight gaps, compliance/regulatory accountability, enforceability of stated guarantees (SLOs, SLAs, data-retention promises). Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-accountability.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-cascade**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Cascade lens. Second- and third-order effects, lock-in, vendor capture, copycat propagation (other teams will mimic this), maintenance debt compounding, cognitive-load tax, supply-chain blast radius, downstream-system impact. Time horizons: 0-6 months, 1-3 years, 5-10 years. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-cascade.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-escalation**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Escalation lens. Narrative of worsening failure over time, cracks → amplification → reckoning. Walks the timeline of how this fails (week 2, month 4, month 12, year 2), not the snapshot. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-escalation.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-integrity**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Integrity lens. Forensic audit of the document's foundational soundness, internal contradictions, evidence quality of the doc's own claims, logical gaps between problem and solution, assumptions stated as facts, numeric claims contradicted by the citation table. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-integrity.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-spectrum**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Spectrum lens. Enforced breadth across feasibility / ethics / governance / human-factor / operational-cost axes. Surfaces axes the document did not consider at all. Single-axis review is the failure this lens catches. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-spectrum.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-sycophancy-judge**: Phase 4 of Litmus. Scores each Phase 3 lens output on five behavioral sycophancy signals. Lenses with scores above the collapse threshold are re-prompted by the orchestrator. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-sycophancy-judge.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-synthesizer**: Phase 5 of Litmus. Reads all prior-phase artifacts and produces report.md per references/report-format.md. No verdict line; evidence-first.
